The Netherlands came back from 2-0 down to earn a 2-2 draw against Greece in the Nations League. Here is our player ratings for the Netherlands players.

Bart Verbruggen 5.5/10: Wasn’t happy with his defence in the first half. Could have done more for the first goal but did make a good save. Had nothing to do after the break.

Denzel Dumfries 5/10: Struggled in the first half before improving in the second. Had two goals disallowed for offside.

Micky van de Ven 5/10: His pace makes up for the gaps he leaves at times and in the firs half he struggled. Like others he got better in the second but had less to do.

Jurrien Timber 4/10: Looked very much like a player who is not fully fit. Second to everything and gave away fouls in the first half. Was too slow too react for the second goal.

Virgil van Dijk 6/10: Defensively he struggled in the first but his goal got his side back into the game after the break.

Joey Veerman 6.5/10: Always looking to make something happen going forward. Not everything worked but was always available. His corner led to a goal an he almost scored a free kick.

Quinten Timber 4/10: Disastrous display as he gave away foul after foul in the first half. Was rightly hooked at half time.

Guus Til 4/10: Like Timber, he offered nothing in the first half and was rightly taken off. Xavi wanted to give him a chance but he didn’t take it.

Crysencio Summerville 6/10: Was Netherlands only outlet going forward in the first half and is a constant threat on the right hand side.

Ruben van Bommel 4.5/10: Was too static at times and his error led to the second goal. Taken off at the break.

Mexx Meerdink 5/10: Tried but was isolated at times. Had no service in the first half and barely touched the ball in the second.

Noa Lang 7/10: Offered more on the left than Van Bommel as he took the game to the Greek defence. His cross led to the equaliser. Likely to start against Serbia after this display.

Tijjani Reijnders 6.5/10: Came on and scored the equaliser with a good finish. Did a lot more running than Til did and it was much needed energy.

Ryan Gravenberch 6.5/10: Immediately helped the Netherlands midfield gain control back in the second half.

Joshua Zirkzee N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

Gjivai Zechiel N/A: Not on the pitch long enough for a review

#2624. Contribute to help victims of the Pedrógão Grande tragedy

Forest fires occur every summer in Portugal but the tragedy that struck the community of Pedrógão Grande in the centre of the country is unprecedented. Over 60 people lost their lives, many trapped in their cars when trying to flee from the flames.

This Just Giving page is a simple way for people, wherever they are in the world, to make a contribution to help rebuild these communities and bring some comfort to those who lost everything in the blink of an eye. The money raised will be sent to a network of local authorities and charities working in the area affected. Thank you for your support.

#2623. Hoeness publicly rebukes teenage gem Karl — ‘He is far from a superstar and needs humility’

By Alexis Pereira | 25 Oct 2026 22:13

Uli Hoeness, Bayern Munich’s honorary president, has issued a public rebuke to 18-year-old forward Lennart Karl, warning that the teenager is far from the superstar status some have attached to him and announcing plans for a ‘serious conversation’ about his development and public profile.

‘He is far from being a superstar,’ Hoeness told Welt am Sonntag. ‘Young players need humility.’ The veteran club figure added that Karl may have been over-protected from scrutiny and that pressure is a necessary part of elite development. ‘I don’t know whether it is good for Lennart Karl to always be over-protected,’ he said.

Hoeness also addressed the comparisons that have been drawn between Karl and Michael Olise, one of Bayern’s established first-team attackers. ‘The comparisons with Olise do not help him at all. There is still a massive difference between them,’ he said. The statement reflects a concern at board level that inflated expectations are creating unhelpful pressure rather than motivation for a teenager still in the early stages of his career.

The Bild newspaper reported separately that Bayern’s hierarchy have been uncomfortable with Karl’s social media presence, specifically the frequency with which he has been sharing content about his personal life and his relationship with lifestyle influencer Zoe Kappele.

The Hoeness intervention comes after a notable dip in form following a breakthrough 2025-26 campaign in which Karl scored nine goals and provided eight assists — including a Champions League goal against Club Brugge in October 2025 that made him the youngest player to score for Bayern in the competition. He was named man of the match in that 4-0 victory at the age of 17.

Those performances generated comparisons with Lionel Messi among some observers, alongside those with Olise. However, a thigh muscle injury sustained during Germany’s pre-World Cup training camp ended his chances of appearing at the tournament, and he has struggled to fully recapture his previous form since returning to fitness.

Hoeness’s public statement is consistent with the club’s tradition of board figures speaking openly about individual players, and is intended as a motivating message rather than a final verdict on Karl’s potential. The key message is that the teenager has not yet reached the level his early emergence suggested to some, and that the path to doing so requires work rather than expectation.

#2622. Pronostico Milan-Bologna, occhio al trend in trasferta della squadra di Palladino

Turno infrasettimanale di Serie A e il Milan torna in campo tre giorni dopo la bella vittoria di Udine. Avversario anch'esso reduce da un successo esterno per 0-2, ossia l'Bologna di Raffaele Palladino che sta lentamente risalendo la china. Due squadre in cerca di continuità, con i rossoneri alla ricerca della terza vittoria consecutiva Si gioca mercoledì 28 ottobre a San Siro alle ore 18.30.

Milan a due facce, quello di questi primi mesi. Ma se in Europa League il percorso è ballerino, in campionato la squadra sta dando buone risposte essendo ancora imbattuta. Non succedeva da 5 anni. Solo tre partite giocate in casa fin qui, tutte vinte segnando almeno 2 reti. Da tenere d'occhio la fascia 61'-69' dove i rossoneri sono particolarmente letali con 9 delle 16 reti realizzate arrivate in questo lasso di tempo.

Bologna che ha vissuto un inizio stagione particolare, con l'esonero di Domenico Tedesco dopo sole 4 giornate. Dall'arrivo di Raffaele Palladino, la squadra ha migliorato la sua situazione vincendo 2 partite, pareggiandone una e perdendone una. Bene soprattutto il cammino in trasferta, dove sono arrivate le uniche due vittorie, sul campo del Lecce e del Cagliari. E proprio nell'ultima partita in Sardegna i rossoblù hanno mantenuto per la prima volta la porta imbattuta.

Partita da gol? I bookies non si sbilanciano troppo. La quota massima per il no gol è di 1.95 mentre il gol è dato a 1.84. I bookmakers che credono in una partita da Over 2.5 che infatti è quotata fino a un massimo di 1.80. L'Under si spinge fino a 2.00.

Turnover inevitabile per Amorim considerato il susseguirsi di impegni ravvicinati e soprattutto il derby da giocare domenica sera. Il tecnico portoghese deve tuttavia convivere con una lista di infortuni che continua ad allungarsi e alla quale si è aggiunto anche Loftus-Cheek. A centrocampo è previsto il riposo di Luka Modric, con Musah e Jashari destinati a prendersi la titolarità del centrocampo, mentre sulla trequarti chance per Alphadjo Cisse alle spalle di Ramos con Pulisic che potrebbe partire dalla panchina. Probabile la conferma del 4-4-1-1.

Palladino senza Orsolini e Piccoli, oltre a Holm che ha finito il 2026. Salvo alcune eccezioni, quello che affronterà il Milan sarà un Bologna molto simile a quello visto contro Inter e Cagliari. Probabile chance per Heggem nel trio di difesa con Casale e Theate. Ballottaggio Miranda-Alhassane sulla corsia di sinistra di cenrocampo. Moro potrebbe far riposare Ferguson in mediana. Davanti sarà conferma per Odgaard come centravanti, preferito a Dovbyk.

Confronto numero 192 tra le due squadre, con 90 vittorie del Milan, 47 pareggi e 54 successi dei felsinei. Limitatamente alle partite giocate a San Siro si registra un trend decisamente favorevole al Milan, che negli ultimi 10 incontri ha vinto 8 volte pareggiando 2. Ultimo successo del Bologna nella Scala del Calcio oltre 10 anni fa, con un gol di Emanuele Giaccherini il 6 gennaio 2016. Il confronto più recente a Milano è stato vinto dal Diavolo, il 14 settembre 2025 con la prima rete in Serie A di Luka Modric.

#2621. Pinamonti con la Lazio a Sassuolo: bronchite e condizioni valutate di ora in ora

La punta ha saltato la vittoria per 2-1 sul Parma e partirà per Reggio Emilia nonostante il problema di salute.

Andrea Pinamonti partirà per Reggio Emilia con la Lazio nonostante la bronchite. I biancocelesti giocheranno domani sul campo del Sassuolo. Le condizioni dell’attaccante saranno valutate di ora in ora nell’avvicinamento alla partita.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Pinamonti ha saltato la precedente gara di campionato della Lazio, vinta per 2-1 contro il Parma. A fermare l’attaccante classe 1999 non è stato un problema di carattere atletico, ma una bronchite che lo limita anche nelle attività quotidiane.

Per il centravanti la prossima sfida ha un significato particolare. Fino all’ultima estate giocava nel Sassuolo, il club che lo ha poi ceduto alla Lazio. Domani tornerà dunque sul campo della sua ex squadra con la maglia biancoceleste.
